# Skills · D · part 14

968 entries, debate to debug-deployment. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **debate**: Use when a technical decision has 2–4 nameable positions and the user wants a recommendation, not open-ended exploration. Triggers include "X vs Y", "A or B", "pick one", "contested trade-off", adversarial review, and decide-between requests. Skip when analysis is open-ended (use superpowers:brainstorming) or the question has no real contest. Keywords: debate, decision, contested, trade-off, versus, steelman, adversarial, argue. ([Lu1sDV/skillsmd](https://github.com/Lu1sDV/skillsmd/tree/HEAD/debate) · ★2)
- **debate**: Multi-model adversarial debate: Claude defends a position against external CLI models (agy/Gemini, Codex, Mistral Vibe). Trigger on "debate this", "challenge my thinking", "devil's advocate", "stress test this idea", "second opinion from other models", "what am I missing". ([nalyk/nalyk-skills](https://github.com/nalyk/nalyk-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/debate/skills/debate) · ★2)
- **debate**: Boule debate council. Form, attack (anonymized), defend or concede, stake-free debiased judge decides. Run as /boule:debate. ([7xuanlu/boule](https://github.com/7xuanlu/boule/tree/HEAD/skills/debate) · ★1)
- **debate**: Have multiple agents argue different positions on a question or decision, then synthesize the strongest answer from their arguments. Use for architecture decisions, strategy choices, or any situation where you want multiple perspectives stress-tested. ([bjorn-ingmanson/thefroject-plugins](https://github.com/bjorn-ingmanson/thefroject-plugins/tree/HEAD/customer-success/skills/debate) · ★1 · 10 copies)
- **debate**: Real Claude vs GPT multi-round debate. Use when you need a second opinion, want to debate architecture decisions, or evaluate competing approaches with multi-model collaboration. ([dr-code/tessera](https://github.com/dr-code/tessera/tree/HEAD/skills/debate) · ★1)
- **debate**: Debate estruturado entre cargos da agência (estrategista, criação, mídia, financeiro, operações, compliance) sobre uma decisão, com consensos e divergências explícitas. Versão leve do Conselho, sem Crítico, Advogado e Sintetizador. Use quando o Marcelo disser "/debate", "quero ver os dois lados disso", "prós e contras de verdade", ou quando duas lentes da agência puxarem para lados opostos. ([firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro](https://github.com/firehagge-ui/HorusOS-Pro/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/debate) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **debate**: Structured adversarial debate for hard decisions. Use when making any technical decision between 2+ options, architecture choices, library selection, REST vs GraphQL, monolith vs microservices, SQL vs NoSQL, or when the user says "should I", "which is better", "compare", "pros and cons", "trade-offs", "help me decide", "A or B", "what approach", or any variation of "I can't decide between". Also use when the user presents two options and seems uncertain which to pick, even if they don't explicitly ask for a debate. ([haytamAroui/AZ-CLAUDE](https://github.com/haytamAroui/AZ-CLAUDE/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/debate) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **debate**: 用于中文辩论备赛、辩题研究、证据搜集与核验、价值辩或政策辩框架、对方论证强化还原、反驳设计、质询、申论稿、结辩稿和材料审计。只要用户提到辩题、正反方、备赛、立论、攻防、证据卡、质询、辩稿或核验辩论资料，就应使用本技能；也适用于只做资料研究或审查现有材料。支持宿主工具、独立模型 API、单路或联合搜索、Crossref/OpenAlex/World Bank，以及基于结构化记录的规则门禁和成稿审计。 ([hellobiyue/debate-prep-workbench](https://github.com/hellobiyue/debate-prep-workbench/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)

- **debate**: Multi-Agent Debate (Solver/Proposer/Critic/Defender/Checker 5エージェント反証) で主張・実装・判断を独立検証。学習源の異なるAI (Claude/Gemini/Codex/Grok+WebSearch) を意図的に対立させてハルシネーション・事実誤認を検出。v1.2 で forecast モード追加: 将来予測系の主張には MiroFish 式の agent-based simulation (ステークホルダー・ペルソナ世界を多ラウンド) でシナリオ分布を生成。v1.3 で precision 強化: Defender(擁護者)を追加し攻撃3:擁護0の非対称を解消、かつ「反論=有罪」をやめ Checker が一次情報で裏取りできた反論のみを採用 (false positive 抑制)。`/debate &lt;主張ファイル or テキスト&gt;` で起動。MARCH/Microsoft CORE研究に基づく実装で false positive -25.8% 期待。 ([AI-Driven-School/aiki](https://github.com/AI-Driven-School/aiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/debate))
